It controls epistemic status and containment, never evidence visibility or presumptive acceptance/rejection.","legacy_docket_assessment":{"adjudicable":false,"packet":null,"status":"invalid"},"packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity":[{"focus_ref":"113:5","identity_status":"legacy_unbound","packet_identity":null,"protocol":"focus-trace-hermetic-response-v4","reader_id":"reader_hft_a","trace_kind":"reconstructed"}]},"reader_synthesis_count":10,"source_present":true,"structured_insight_count":15,"unstructured_record_count":0},"identity":{"ayah_ref":"113:5","lane":"global","linguistic_source_ref":"113:5","surface_ref":"113:5","unit_kind":"numbered_ayah"},"primary_floor":{"source_ref":"113:5","target_tokens":[["ve",["113:5:1"]],["kıskanan",["113:5:3"]],["kıskandığında",["113:5:4","113:5:5"]],["onun",["113:5:3"]],["kötülüğünden",["113:5:1","113:5:2"]]],"text":"ve kıskanan kıskandığında onun kötülüğünden.\""},"schema_version":"commentary-v5-hermetic-scope-packet-v1","scope":{"hft":{"assigned_record_count":10,"authoring_policy":{"every_assigned_record_requires_explicit_review":true,"parseable_hft_is_always_visible_to_authoring":true,"provenance_affects_qualification_not_visibility":true,"scope_mismatch_affects_lane_assignment_not_visibility":true,"unresolved_branch_citations_do_not_suppress_records":true},"authoring_status":"visible_with_provenance_qualification"},"lane_contract":"reader walks and cross-run publications with explicit trust labels","pericope":{"ayah_from":1,"ayah_to":5,"id":"s113-p01-001-005","label":"Whole surah","number":1,"refs":["113:1","113:2","113:3","113:4","113:5"]},"readiness":{"authoring_effect":"Docket readiness is reported for source provenance. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2d45cd8998ac4ff459ae","text":"No later ayah follows 113:5, so no later textual activation occurred after it. On full rereading, however, the final ayah retroactively gathered earlier mechanisms into one human source: `غ س ق` supplied darkened seeing, `ن ف ث` supplied outward venting, `ع ق د` supplied fixed or knotted intent, and `ق و ل` supplied inner or circulating speech. The surprise at the end is that the envier can be the personal condensation of the whole prior sequence: hidden darkness becoming an enacted attempt to remove another's good.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_387abf8e3905d0422e30","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:5:3\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\"]],[\"113:5:5\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kıskançlık, ortaya çıkarılmış ve beslenmiş iyiliği geri almaya çalışan, koruyucu bakımın karşısındaki bir eksiltme girişimi olarak görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001","root_000319/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3b09ab0d288beac99f07","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:5:3\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"113:5:5\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Karşılaştırma iki yöne ayrılır: benzer bir iyiliği istemek tek başına bu kötülük değildir; tehdit, ötekinin iyiliğinin yok olmasını istemekle belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3e16d2802df8445488f7","text":"3. **Envy is an anti-nurture project aimed at undoing bestowed good.** Fixed **ح س د B001** gives the wished or attempted removal of a blessing. The surah begins with **ر ح م B001** beneficence, then names **ر ب ب B002** nurture and completion, **ف ل ق B003** the bringing forth of created things, and **خ ل ق B002** originating creation. The packet supplies a produced, nurtured benefit and a will to subtract it; I infer that another's flourishing is the object connecting those poles. The final ayah thus reads as a social counter-project to the surah's opening movement of mercy, creation, and preservation, rather than as a private feeling with no causal reach.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_49ad0be13092c1b6886f","text":"No later ayah exists in the boundary-clipped focus window, so no later-ayah surprise emerged.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4daf91adf71cdd523d16","text":"3. **Competitive upward imitation at the boundary of harm.** Evidence: `ح س د B002` emulative envy without dispossession; activated by `س م و B007` rivaling by vying upward, `س م و B008` good repute spread, and `ش ر ر B011` quarreling. Mechanism: the inventory preserves a non-dispossessive branch of envy, while the phrase `مِن شَرِّ` marks the dangerous conversion of comparison into injury. I infer that the ayah protects at the threshold where emulation becomes removal-seeking or quarrel. Reading change: the repetition `حَاسِدٍ إِذَا حَسَدَ` reads as activation: not every comparison is yet harm, but the envier is dangerous when envy is enacted.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6c9f56898a8d9931ab23","text":"4. **Earlier hidden and binding harms can become modalities of enacted envy.** Fixed **ح س د B001** explicitly allows active pursuit of another's loss. Earlier 113:3 supplies concealed entry through **و ق ب B002**, and 113:4 supplies subtle emission through **ن ف ث B001**, physical binding through **ع ق د B001**, and spell-binding through **ع ق د B013**. The packet does not identify one agent across these ayat; I supply the abductive possibility that concealment, insinuation, or binding are means by which removal-seeking envy can act. On this retained reading, *إِذَا حَسَدَ* retrospectively gathers the preceding threats into possible operations of a hostile social intention without reducing them to that intention.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6f28b6b62dd4870db4de","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:5:3\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\"]],[\"113:5:5\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İçteki giderme isteği söz, üfleme, bağlama veya gizli giriş gibi dış kanallar bulduğunda, önceki tehditler kıskançlığın uygulanma yollarına dönüşebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_83c04cd96fe7117581cd","text":"2. **From visual comparison to darkened perception.** Evidence: `ح س د B001` seeing another's blessing and wishing it removed; activated by `غ س ق B003` eye becoming darkened and `ع و ذ B002` protective charm/invocation from evil eye, fear, illness, or harm. Mechanism: the packet supplies a visual trigger for envy and a branch where the eye loses brightness. I infer that envy darkens perception before it becomes action. Reading change: the final ayah is not only about social rivalry; it is the moral darkening of sight focused on another's good.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_89e8fec158b572ed7079","text":"4. **Hidden intention becoming performative force.** Evidence: `ح س د B001` desire/sometimes active seeking of removal; activated by `ع ق د B006/B012` fixed conviction and knotted anger, `ن ف ث B003` venting what is in the chest, `ق و ل B012` unspoken inner saying, and `ق و ل B007` circulating report. Mechanism: earlier ayat build a progression from inner saying to breath, knot, and public report. I infer that envy becomes harmful when an interior contraction finds an outward channel. Reading change: the final ayah gathers the surah's previous mechanisms into a human source: dark interior will becoming speech, breath, knot, or social circulation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_aae545e7fe7dd6bc6708","text":"5. **Upward comparison can fork into emulation or subtraction.** Fixed **ح س د B002** supplies non-dispossessive emulation and fixed **ح س د B001** dispossessive envy. The opening *اسم* carries dormant **س م و B007**, rivaling by vying upward, and **س م و B005**, raised designation; the final ayah closes the packet with a person reacting to another's visible blessing. The packet supplies upward rivalry and the two directions of envy; I infer comparison as the shared pivot that can seek either a like good or the removal of the other's good. The fixed repetition *حَاسِدٍ ... حَسَدَ* reads as the point where comparison takes its harmful, subtractive branch while the emulative branch remains visibly distinct.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b1f6c865cbd9bb85e001","text":"1. **The conditional marks the crossing from disposition to harmful enactment.** Fixed **ح س د B001** includes seeing another's blessing, wishing its removal, and possibly acting to remove it. The same root appears first as agent *حَاسِد* and then as finite act *حَسَدَ* under *إِذَا*, matching 113:3's actor/state plus conditional event. The packet supplies the disposition, actor, action, and repeated conditional pattern; I infer that the threat becomes acute when inward orientation turns operative. The ayah therefore reads dynamically: refuge is sought from removal-seeking envy at its moment of activation, not from a static label attached to a person.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c0848a5870ac8129915f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:5:3\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\"]],[\"113:5:5\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Koşul yapısı, içteki kıskanç eğilim ile onun başkasının iyiliğini gidermeye yönelik eyleme dönüşmesini ayırır; tehlike bu geçişte keskinleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c79e4b93503bfdbe27f0","text":"1. **Envy as attempted removal of mercy-gift.** Evidence: `ح س د B001` wanting another's blessing removed; activated by `ر ح م B001` beneficence toward the one shown mercy, `ر ب ب B002` nurture/completion, and `خ ل ق B005` aptness/being fitted for a thing. Mechanism: the packet gives blessings as mercy, nurture, formation, and aptness, then names a subject who wants such benefit stripped from another. I infer that the envier's harm targets the recipient's divinely sustained good, not merely the envier's private feeling. Reading change: `حَاسِدٍ إِذَا حَسَدَ` reads as a threat against bestowed flourishing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c9c349b09cffc1cda522","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"113:5:3\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\"]],[\"113:5:5\",\"root_000319\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Başkasındaki iyiliğe bakış, eylemden önce algıyı karartan ve görülen iyiliği hedefe dönüştüren ahlaki bir bozulma yaşayabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000319/B002","root_000787/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d23cb236199f9fcbb099","text":"2. **The non-dispossessive branch corrects an overbroad account of the threat.** Fixed **ح س د B002** supplies emulative desire for a like blessing without wishing the owner to lose it, whereas fixed **ح س د B001** supplies dispossession and removal. Their shared root is placed under **ش ر ر B001** and repeated as action. The packet supplies both accepted branches and the explicit harmful frame; I infer that direction toward another's loss, not comparison alone, is what makes the mechanism cohere. B002 therefore functions as a lexical boundary: the fixed ayah does not require every wish for comparable good to operate by the evil mechanism described in B001.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The noun-plus-إِذَا-plus-verb construction, reinforced by 113:3, moves the reading from fixed evil identity to a threshold where a disposition enters effective action.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_ffe8b07c4e06829ff2b3","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
